## <ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>SIAO</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>HYIN-LI</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>TENG</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>GYI-GO</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>TI-ʽÔ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>NYING</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>BU-ZI</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>嘉慶</rb><rt>Kyüô-kyʽing</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天竺</rb><rt>Tʽin-coh</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姓數</rb><rt>sing-su</rt><rb>姓</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>羅</rb><rt>Lo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名</rb><rt>ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>亭那巴</rb><rt>Ding-na-pô</rt><rb>縣</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>武官</rb><rt>vu-kwun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>打煞了</rb><rt>tang-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗上</rb><rt>Feh-zông</rt><rb>週歲</rb><rt>tsiu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喪掉了</rb><rt>sông-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>將以</rb><rt>tsiang-yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起來</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>嘸阿爹</rb><rt>m-ah-tia</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>託託</rb><rt>tʽôh-tʽôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>膽量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>頭遭子</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>應承了</rb><rt>ing-dzing-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>失約個</rb><rt>shih-iah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸阿爹個</rb><rt>m-ah-tia-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>應承了個</rb><rt>ing-dzing-liao-go</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶利米</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-mi</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>四十九</rb><rt>s-jih-kyiu</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十一</rb><rt>jih-ih</rt><rb>節裏</rb><rt>tsih-li</rt><rb>寫落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾儂個</rb><rt>Ng-noh-go</rt><rb>嘸阿爹</rb><rt>m-ah-tia</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>寄東</rb><rt>kyi-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>爾儂個</rb><rt>Ng-noh-go</rt><rb>孤孀老𡢿</rb><rt>kwu-sông-lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>一記</rb><rt>ih-kyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>住勒</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>亭那巴</rb><rt>Ding-na-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>巴得那</rb><rt>Pô-teh-na</rt><rb>兩縣個</rb><rt>liang-yün-go</rt><rb>交界</rb><rt>kao-ka</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>一間</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>着實</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>低下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一種</rb><rt>ih-cong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>寄拜</rb><rt>kyi-pa</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>抱來仔</rb><rt>bao-læ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>好仔</rb><rt>hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嚌奏</rb><rt>tsi-tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>小小管</rb><rt>siao-siao-kwun</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>話弗出</rb><rt>wô-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>小苦</rb><rt>siao-kwʽu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫到了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tao-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>虧勒</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>轎夫</rb><rt>gyiao-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做過了</rb><rt>tso-ko-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一直落來</rb><rt>ih-dzih-lôh-læ</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>轎夫</rb><rt>gyiao-fu</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>粥</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>燉燉</rb><rt>teng-teng</rt><rb>熱</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>籮窠</rb><rt>lo-kʽo</rt><rb>拐拐</rb><rt>kwa-kwa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脫</rb><rt>tʽah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一色</rb><rt>ih-seh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭一句</rb><rt>deo-ih-kyü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗上</rb><rt>Feh-zông</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>蒲齊個</rb><rt>Bu-zi-go</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>小小管</rb><rt>siao-siao-kwun</rt><rb>雪白憐</rb><rt>shih-bah-lin</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擄擄</rb><rt>lo-lo</rt><rb>蒲齊個</rb><rt>Bu-zi-go</rt><rb>黑黑</rb><rt>heh-heh</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩年</rb><rt>liang-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>Jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>走開</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>夜到頭</rb><rt>Yia-tao-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>蓆子</rb><rt>zih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>鋪好仔</rb><rt>pʽu-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>睏東</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牛奶</rb><rt>ngeo-na</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饅頭茶</rb><rt>mun-deo-dzô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>備</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>好東</rb><rt>hao-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>出個</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>車子裏</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts-li</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>花園裏</rb><rt>hwô-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嬉戲</rb><rt>hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>抱進來</rb><rt>Bao-tsing-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>穿穿</rb><rt>cʽün-cʽün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後首</rb><rt>ʽeo-siu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>兩覺</rb><rt>liang-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>Ih-nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嬲嬲和</rb><rt>na-na-ʽo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>抱抱</rb><rt>bao-bao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>肩胛頭</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>背背</rb><rt>pe-pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>氈條毛毯</rb><rt>tsin-diao-mao-tʽæn</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>𨀤來</rb><rt>le-læ</rt><rb>𨀤去</rb><rt>le-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>外撥</rb><rt>nga-peh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>兩三</rb><rt>liang-sæn</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生了</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>一場</rb><rt>ih-dziang</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>怕怕</rb><rt>pʽô-pʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>一場</rb><rt>ih-dziang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人個</rb><rt>siao-nying-go</rt><rb>底子</rb><rt>ti-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>扎注</rb><rt>tsah-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>生病間</rb><rt>sang-bing-kæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>離身</rb><rt>li-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>瘥了</rb><rt>tsʽô-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>話起來</rb><rt>wô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別人</rb><rt>Bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>睬其</rb><rt>tsʽæ-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咭咧骨勒</rb><rt>kyih-lih-kweh-leh</rt><rb>咭咧骨勒</rb><rt>kyih-lih-kweh-leh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>歹歹</rb><rt>tæ-tæ</rt><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽勒出個</rb><rt>tʽing-leh-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>外堂裏</rb><rt>Nga-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>糖</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>蜜煎</rb><rt>mih-tsin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽA</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>襪</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>大腳褲</rb><rt>do-kyiah-kwʽu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>腳裏</rb><rt>kyiah-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>腳鐲</rb><rt>kyiah-gyüoh</rt><rb>戴的</rb><rt>ta-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>看得出</rb><rt>kʽen-teh-cʽih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>Tsih-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>黃黃</rb><rt>wông-wông</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>藍陰陰</rb><rt>læn-ing-ing</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有數</rb><rt>yiu-su</rt><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一國</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬抬</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看慣了個</rb><rt>kʽen-kwæn-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亭那巴</rb><rt>Ding-na-pô</rt><rb>禮拜堂裏</rb><rt>li-pa-dông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多個</rb><rt>hyü-to-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亭那巴</rb><rt>Ding-na-pô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜個</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>泥塑木雕</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>恆沙</rb><rt>ʽEng-sô</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃個</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>其道</rb><rt>gyi-dao</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>回回</rb><rt>We-we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>教會了</rb><rt>kao-we-de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>怪</rb><rt>kwa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗來個</rb><rt>feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>去世個</rb><rt>kyʽü-shü-go</rt><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教門裏</rb><rt>kyiao-meng-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>接歸</rb><rt>tsih-kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>摜</rb><rt>gwæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>新來個</rb><rt>sing-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>ôh-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一落過了</rb><rt>ih-lôh-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘個</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧師</rb><rt>moh-s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>教勒</rb><rt>kao-leh</rt><rb>規規</rb><rt>kwe-kwe</rt><rb>矩矩</rb><rt>kyü-kyü</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>箱子</rb><rt>siang-ts</rt><rb>帶來</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>本數</rb><rt>peng-su</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>含</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡好看</rb><rt>ôh-hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小小人</rb><rt>siao-siao-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弄堂裏</rb><rt>long-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>團團</rb><rt>dön-dön</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>不忍相</rb><rt>peh-jing-siang</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揀擇</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh</rt><rb>幾張</rb><rt>kyi-tsiang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt><rb>花綠紙</rb><rt>hwô-loh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擺</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地板頂</rb><rt>di-pæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>門口頭</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>坐間個</rb><rt>zo-kæn-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>張張</rb><rt>tsiang-tsiang</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>花綠紙</rb><rt>hwô-loh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>陪勒</rb><rt>be-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>勢微</rb><rt>shü-vi</rt><rb>熟了點了</rb><rt>joh-leh-tin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>兩日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>獨自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>惡歡喜</rb><rt>ôh-hwun-hyi</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>嘸多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 vong-li</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>憂悶相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>難事</rb><rt>næn-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>花綠紙</rb><rt>hwô-loh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>其道</rb><rt>gyi-dao</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇字</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇字</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>點向</rb><rt>tin-hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>嘸幾日</rb><rt>m-kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>謳出</rb><rt>eo-cʽih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>帶來個</rb><rt>ta-læ-go</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>認</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>廿六個</rb><rt>nyiæn-loh-go</rt><rb>字母</rb><rt>z-meo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拼字個</rb><rt>pʽing-z-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拼</rb><rt>pʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>依邊</rb><rt>i-pin</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依邊</rb><rt>i-pin</rt><rb>口傳</rb><rt>kʽeo-djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等弗到</rb><rt>teng-feh-tao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》。<ruby><rb>第一樣</rb><rt>Di-ih-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>東西個</rb><rt>tong-si-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>死後</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>死後</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>壞壞</rb><rt>w̆a-w̆a</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>Kyiao-leh</rt><rb>地獄</rb><rt>di-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>哥羅西</rb><rt>Ko-lo-si</rt><rb>書〉裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>十六</rb><rt>jih-loh</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>着一記</rb><rt>djôh-ih-kyi</rt><rb>明白了</rb><rt>ming-bah-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>唉</rb><rt>Æ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>說話的了</rb><rt>shih-wô-tih-de</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>騙</rb><rt>pʽin</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿姆</rb><rt>a-m</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>仔</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔出去</rb><rt>peng-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弄堂裏</rb><rt>long-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>簇嶄新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哄</rb><rt>hong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關好仔</rb><rt>kwæn-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>咪咪笑</rb><rt>mi-mi-siao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>忘記了</rb><rt>mông-kyi-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>跪帶倒</rb><rt>gyü-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>智慧</rb><rt>cü-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>瓦</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>燒出來個</rb><rt>siao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>摜勒</rb><rt>gwæn-leh</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>百木粉碎</rb><rt>pah-moh-feng-se</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>問勒</rb><rt>meng-leh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>保</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>十聲</rb><rt>jih-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>Wô</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>各到</rb><rt>kôh-tao</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>忖的個</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從來</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世</rb><rt>üong-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>味道個</rb><rt>mi-dao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>盤問</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>歇工了</rb><rt>hyih-kong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>Hyiao-teh</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>暴時</rb><rt>bao-z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>嘸過</rb><rt>m-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>夏娃</rb><rt>Yüô-w̆ô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>魔鬼個</rb><rt>Mo-kwe-go</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-leh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>罪惡個</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>發作</rb><rt>fah-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>第一代個</rb><rt>di-ih-dæ-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>本性</rb><rt>peng-sing</rt><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>變掉了</rb><rt>pin-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>生出來</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 siang-miao</sup>

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問勒</rb><rt>meng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>惡愫個</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>喏</rb><rt>Nô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>母老</rb><rt>meo-lao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>Iao-bông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>扇子</rb><rt>sin-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>扇</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>扇</rb><rt>sin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>打來</rb><rt>tang-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇向</rb><rt>keh-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別向</rb><rt>bih-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>Tsih-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>犯難</rb><rt>væn-næn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>年數</rb><rt>nyin-su</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歹</rb><rt>tæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>辨弗出</rb><rt>bin-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>虛話</rb><rt>hyü-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偷了</rb><rt>tʽeo-leh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>打個</rb><rt>tang-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>詳詳</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>細細</rb><rt>si-si</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>其道</rb><rt>gyi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>弄環了</rb><rt>long-wæn-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>首</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>教過仔</rb><rt>kao-ko-ts</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教過仔</rb><rt>kao-ko-ts</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>熟熟管</rb><rt>joh-joh-kwun</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>上主</rb><rt>Zông-Cü</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>岔</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>鏖糟八剌</rb><rt>ao-tsao-pah-la</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>行善</rb><rt>ʽang-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世個</rb><rt>üong-si-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt><rb>前書</rb><rt>zin-shü</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>六章</rb><rt>loh-tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>惡人</rb><rt>Ôh-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>馬太傳</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa-djün</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>廿五</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四十六</rb><rt>s-jih-loh</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世</rb><rt>üong-si</rt><rb>刑罰個</rb><rt>ying-vah-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>教了</rb><rt>kao-leh</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世個</rb><rt>üong-si-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩伐</rb><rt>liang-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>？」

<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>教向其道</rb><rt>kao-hyiang-gyi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>Tʽin-zông</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>投胎</rb><rt>deo-tʽæ</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>苦難</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>釘勒</rb><rt>ting-leh</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三日</rb><rt>di-sæn-nyih</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂裏</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪愆個</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin-go</rt><rb>惡報</rb><rt>ôh-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受過了</rb><rt>ziu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世個</rb><rt>üong-si-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>天堂裏</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông-li</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世個</rb><rt>üong-si-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十六</rb><rt>jih-loh</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>哥羅西</rb><rt>Ko-lo-si</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>廿</rb><rt>nyiæn</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>。）

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>講出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>做忌</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗歇</rb><rt>Feh-hyih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇樣</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做仔</rb><rt>tso-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt><rb>有限</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做人個</rb><rt>tso-nying-go</rt><rb>行止</rb><rt>ʽang-ts</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>壞</rb><rt>W̆a</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>句</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>怏</rb><rt>ông</rt><rb>煞了</rb><rt>sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>和和睦睦</rb><rt>ʽo-ʽo-moh-moh</rt><rb>客客氣氣</rb><rt>kʽah-kʽah-kyʽi-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>Z-min-zông</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>窮</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>作作</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh</rt><rb>揖</rb><rt>ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>總要</rb><rt>tsong-iao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>對開</rb><rt>te-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耍貨</rb><rt>shô-ho</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總要</rb><rt>tsong-iao</rt><rb>兌勒</rb><rt>de-leh</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>候數</rb><rt>ʽeo-su</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tô-fah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>苦個</rb><rt>kwʽu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 ze-bih</sup>

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>大姑娘個</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>一箱子</rb><rt>ih-siang-ts</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>整出</rb><rt>tsing-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>書架裏</rb><rt>shü-kô-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>安的</rb><rt>en-tih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>整</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>幫襯</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>面子</rb><rt>min-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>板子</rb><rt>pæn-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做樣個</rb><rt>tso-yiang-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讀讀</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂裏</rb><rt>tʽin-dông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>救世主個</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-Yiang</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>哦㗇</rb><rt>Ô-yüô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嬉戲個</rb><rt>hyi-hyi-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>坐個</rb><rt>zo-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>咪咪笑</rb><rt>mi-mi-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>甮了</rb><rt>Vong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甮了</rb><rt>vong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>嬉戲</rb><rt>hyi-hyi</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>馱東</rb><rt>do-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨爾</rb><rt>ze-ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>送撥</rb><rt>song-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>謳帶進來</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>桃紅</rb><rt>dao-ʽong</rt><rb>書面</rb><rt>shü-min</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>寶藍</rb><rt>pao-læn</rt><rb>書面</rb><rt>shü-min</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>揀好仔</rb><rt>kæn-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>綢</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱起</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>裁縫師父</rb><rt>zæ-vong-s-vu</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>袋</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>簇嶄新</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》。<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚頭</rb><rt>mæn-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讀讀</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。〈<ruby><rb>創世記</rb><rt>Tsʽông-shü-kyi</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>讀勒</rb><rt>doh-leh</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>嘸多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一章</rb><rt>ih-tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一節</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>讀勒出</rb><rt>doh-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>費掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>從小</rb><rt>dzong-siao</rt><rb>嘸爹孃個</rb><rt>m-tia-nyiang-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>暗力薄朧</rb><rt>en-lih-boh-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一色</rb><rt>ih-seh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得了</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>別開個</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>近攏來了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>一忖</rb><rt>ih-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>傷心起來了</rb><rt>sông-sing-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>Feh-zing</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一問</rb><rt>ih-meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書》裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用個</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>馬可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>nyi</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby>。）「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三位一體</rb><rt>Sæn-We-Ih-Tʽi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>We-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>父</rb><rt>Vu</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt></ruby>。」
