「<ruby><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書》裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>看出來</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>福音書</rb><rt>foh-ing-shü</rt></ruby>〉<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>Kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」

「<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>Hao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>一節</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>造出來個</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好好個</rb><rt>hao-hao-go</rt></ruby>。」

「<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>消</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>市裏個</rb><rt>z-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>唉</rb><rt>Æ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>省</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>壞法</rb><rt>w̆a-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>近近</rb><rt>gying-gying</rt><rb>屋裏邊</rb><rt>oh-li-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看勒出</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽÔ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人客</rb><rt>nyü-nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>廳裏</rb><rt>tʽing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿姆</rb><rt>a-m</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>阿姆</rb><rt>a-m</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>Z-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可憐相</rb><rt>kʽo-lin-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>哄</rb><rt>hong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>背後</rb><rt>pe-ʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好起來</rb><rt>hao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>答應</rb><rt>teh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大廳裏</rb><rt>do-tʽing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>嘸幾個</rb><rt>M-kyi-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>謊話</rb><rt>hwông-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>昨日子</rb><rt>zô-nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>車子裏</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts-li</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馬伕</rb><rt>mô-fu</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>騎</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>馬伕</rb><rt>mô-fu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一遍</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>靠弗實</rb><rt>kʽao-feh-jih</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-teh</rt><rb>棒</rb><rt>bông</rt><rb>馱的</rb><rt>do-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打痛</rb><rt>tang-tʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥我</rb><rt>peh-ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>下遭</rb><rt>ʽô-tsao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用剩個</rb><rt>yüong-dzing-go</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>恕恕</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嗐</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>壞法</rb><rt>w̆a-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>嗐</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>Do-do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>Væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>惡人</rb><rt>Ôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕落</rb><rt>ken-lôh</rt><rb>地獄</rb><rt>di-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>國數個</rb><rt>koh-su-go</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby>〉19, 17. 

「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕落</rb><rt>ken-lôh</rt><rb>地獄</rb><rt>di-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>避勒過</rb><rt>bi-leh-ko</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救個</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇節</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得救</rb><rt>teh-kyiu</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>使徒行傳</rb><rt>S-du-ying-djün</rt></ruby>〉16, 31. 

「<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」

「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>Z-jün</rt><rb>𣍐個</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>看弗過</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>解掉</rb><rt>ka-diao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>書信</rb><rt>shü-sing</rt></ruby>·<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby>〉1: 17. 〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>書信</rb><rt>shü-sing</rt></ruby>·<ruby><rb>第一封</rb><rt>di-ih-fong</rt></ruby>〉3: 3. 

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>Sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>可憐相個</rb><rt>kʽo-lin-siang-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一坒</rb><rt>ih-bi</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>讚</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驕傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>低微心個</rb><rt>ti-vi-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby> 〈<ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby>〉4. 6. <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>簇嶄新</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>忖個</rb><rt>tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壞個</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>要緊</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>水果</rb><rt>shü-ko</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>糖</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>饞癆</rb><rt>dzæn-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>糖</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>水果</rb><rt>shü-ko</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>句</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>假話</rb><rt>kô-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>性格</rb><rt>sing-kah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驕傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>快勒</rb><rt>kwʽa-leh</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>sang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>歡喜弗煞</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-feh-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>Ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解樹螂</rb><rt>ka-djü-lông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>硬心</rb><rt>ngang-sing</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嬲嬲和</rb><rt>na-na-ʽo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>變動</rb><rt>pin-dong</rt><rb>起來了</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一眼眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-ngæn</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt><rb>糖</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>掃</rb><rt>sao</rt><rb>火爐</rb><rt>ho-lu</rt><rb>師父個</rb><rt>s-vu-go</rt><rb>徒弟</rb><rt>du-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>Tsih-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>Ng-noh</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>調覺</rb><rt>diao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>鏡乾</rb><rt>kying-ken</rt><rb>雪淨</rb><rt>shih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>引導</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。 〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby>〉14: 17. <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>房裏個</rb><rt>vông-li-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關好了</rb><rt>kwæn-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>跪帶落</rb><rt>gyü-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>乾淨個</rb><rt>ken-zing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>復轉</rb><rt>voh-cün</rt><rb>端正個</rb><rt>tön-tsing-go</rt><rb>念頭</rb><rt>nyiæn-deo</rt></ruby>。 〈<ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby>〉51, 12.

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>好仔</rb><rt>hao-ts</rt><rb>爬起來了</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>嗅嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>嘸幾</rb><rt>m-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只會</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>呆頓頓</rb><rt>ngæ-teng-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>話了</rb><rt>wô-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>哦㗇</rb><rt>Ô-üô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>話起</rb><rt>wô-kyʽi</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>Zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變了</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>壞人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸爹孃</rb><rt>m-tia-nyiang</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby>〉14: 18 

「<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>慈悲個</rb><rt>dz-pe-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾過了</rb><rt>ng-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸爹孃</rb><rt>m-tia-nyiang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>外教人</rb><rt>nga-kyiao-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>咋樣</rb><rt>dza-nyiang</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾儂</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒走轉來</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>地獄裏個</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li-go</rt><rb>路頭</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>箇打</rb><rt>keh-tang</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>教教</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一冊</rb><rt>ih-tsʽah</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>叫</rb><rt>Kyiao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。」（〈<ruby><rb>帖撒</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah</rt></ruby>〉5: 23, 24.）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>首</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>去仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-ts</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一節</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>唱唱</rb><rt>tsʽông-tsʽông</rt><rb>記</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>首</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>迷路</rb><rt>mi-lu</rt><rb>嘸做個</rb><rt>m-tso-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，

<ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby>，

<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死路</rb><rt>si-lu</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，

<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>寶貝</rb><rt>pao-pe</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>Do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>四塊</rb><rt>s-kwʽe</rt><rb>番餅</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別開</rb><rt>Bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>Jün</rt><rb>一記</rb><rt>ih-kyi</rt><rb>開了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>上岸</rb><rt>zông-ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>撐過</rb><rt>tsʽang-ko</rt><rb>江裏向</rb><rt>kông-li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>灣</rb><rt>w̆æn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從此以後</rb><rt>Dzong-tsʽ-yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>頭遭子</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但</rb><rt>Dæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>讀會了</rb><rt>doh-we-liao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世</rb><rt>üong-si</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做做</rb><rt>tso-tso</rt><rb>隊</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外堂裏</rb><rt>nga-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗得知</rb><rt>feh-teh-cü</rt><rb>心焦</rb><rt>sing-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>歡喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>所話個</rb><rt>sô-wô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忖弗出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>熱心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>泥塑木雕個</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>虛花個</rb><rt>hyü-hwô-go</rt><rb>行當</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>特特爲爲</rb><rt>deh-deh-we-we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊個</rb><rt>Bu-zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>變化弗來個</rb><rt>pin-hwô-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>忖頭</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神個</rb><rt>Jing-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>低聲小意</rb><rt>ti-sing-siao-i</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>成功</rb><rt>dzing-kong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>抲定主意</rb><rt>kʽô-ding-cü-i</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>代等</rb><rt>dæ-teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>起來了</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>Gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>丟掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>夜</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗對個</rb><rt>feh-te-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>Hyü-to</rt><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>流過來個</rb><rt>liu-ko-læ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>教門</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>回回</rb><rt>We-we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>佛</rb><rt>Veh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯勒</rb><rt>væn-leh</rt><rb>重罪</rb><rt>djong-ze</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>恆沙河</rb><rt>ʽEng-sô-ʽo</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>阻擋</rb><rt>tsu-tông</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>駁倒</rb><rt>poh-tao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>總是</rb><rt>tsong-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>禱告文</rb><rt>tao-kao-veng</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>好仔</rb><rt>hao-ts</rt></ruby>；《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>查查</rb><rt>dzô-dzô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有時節</rb><rt>Yiu-z-tsin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>坐帶攏</rb><rt>zo-tæn-long</rt><rb>清盯盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting-ting</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>哦㗇</rb><rt>Ô-üô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>賺</rb><rt>dzæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>指向</rb><rt>ts-hyiang</rt><rb>爾道</rb><rt>ng-dao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>正路</rb><rt>tsing-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。」（〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby>〉14: 6.）<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>Wô</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>投胎</rb><rt>deo-tʽæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>死來</rb><rt>si-læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活轉來了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天父個</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>oh-ziang</rt><rb>弗大裏</rb><rt>feh-da-li</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>笑</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>嘸要得緊</rb><rt>m-iao-teh-kying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>心急</rb><rt>sing-kyih</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>生氣</rb><rt>sang-kyʽi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>𣍐個</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>遢</rb><rt>tʽah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>黃梅</rb><rt>wông-me</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正是</rb><rt>tsing-z</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>茂盛</rb><rt>meo-zing</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>加勒葛</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-keh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>適意</rb><rt>shih-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>駛過</rb><rt>s-ko</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>Me-nyih</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>lôh-sæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>錨</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>拋落了</rb><rt>pʽao-lôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上岸</rb><rt>zông-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田野畈裏</rb><rt>din-yia-pæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>乘乘</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>風涼</rb><rt>fong-liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>暴時</rb><rt>bao-z</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>愚蠢</rb><rt>nyü-cʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>lôh-sæn</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>冷冷</rb><rt>lang-lang</rt><rb>陰陰</rb><rt>ing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>上岸</rb><rt>zông-ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬上</rb><rt>bô-zông</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>景緻</rb><rt>kying-cü</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>恆沙河</rb><rt>ʽEng-sô-ʽo</rt><rb>彎彎</rb><rt>w̆æn-w̆æn</rt><rb>曲曲</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烏篷</rb><rt>u-bong</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>沿</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>泊潮</rb><rt>boh-dziao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>綠㿮㿮</rb><rt>loh-ang-ang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩</rb><rt>dông</rt><rb>半邊</rb><rt>pun-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>稻田</rb><rt>dao-din</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>竹</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>園</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>芭蕉</rb><rt>pô-tsiao</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>棕櫚</rb><rt>tsong-li</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>煮煮</rb><rt>ts-ts</rt><rb>夜飯</rb><rt>yia-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>搭地</rb><rt>tah-di</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>菸</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>久了</rb><rt>kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗全</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>蕩𡍲</rb><rt>dông-tʽah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>和和睦睦</rb><rt>ʽo-ʽo-moh-moh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>禮拜堂</rb><rt>li-pa-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》；<ruby><rb>禮拜日子</rb><rt>li-pa-nyih-ts</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>禮拜堂裏</rb><rt>li-pa-dông-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>哦㗇</rb><rt>ô-üô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>志向</rb><rt>ts-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>教門</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>富貴</rb><rt>fu-kwe</rt><rb>貧賤個</rb><rt>bing-zin-go</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>𡍲</rb><rt>tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嗐</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>虎豹</rb><rt>hwu-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>忒</rb><rt>tʽeh</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>mang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>嘸幾時</rb><rt>m-kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>喫𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>Dza-go</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>官人</rb><rt>kwun-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大英國</rb><rt>Da-Ing-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>說</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大英國</rb><rt>Da-Ing-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>腰縛裏</rb><rt>iao-bo-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>痛起來</rb><rt>tʽong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>嘸幾時</rb><rt>m-kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>哦㗇</rb><rt>Ô-üô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋樣</rb><rt>dza-nyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吸上</rb><rt>hyih-zông</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>可憐相</rb><rt>Kʽo-lin-siang</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>苦苦</rb><rt>kwʽu-kwʽu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗相干</rb><rt>feh-siang-ken</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>個把</rb><rt>ko-pô</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>加勒葛</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-keh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姓數</rb><rt>sing-su</rt><rb>姓</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>亭那巴</rb><rt>Ding-na-pô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>鬧熱</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>時道</rb><rt>z-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>往</rb><rt>w̆ông</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>忙勒緊</rb><rt>mông-leh-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>忘記了</rb><rt>mông-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>Se-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腰脅</rb><rt>iao-hyih</rt><rb>痛</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>痛勒厲害</rb><rt>tʽong-leh-li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩遭</rb><rt>liang-tsao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>面色</rb><rt>min-seh</rt><rb>白䩍䩍</rb><rt>bah-liao-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>嘸神氣</rb><rt>m-jing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話過了</rb><rt>wô-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗在乎</rb><rt>feh-dzæ-wu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>奢華</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>mao-bing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>首飾</rb><rt>siu-sih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>聽勒</rb><rt>tʽing-leh</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽勒</rb><rt>tʽing-leh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>覅其</rb><rt>fæ-gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坐起</rb><rt>zo-kyʽi</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>門口頭</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>路梯</rb><rt>lu-tʽæ</rt><rb>弄裏</rb><rt>long-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>腳䯊頭</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>馱的</rb><rt>do-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>背脊</rb><rt>pe-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>箇向</rb><rt>keh-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>靜靜</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>𠲎</rb><rt>fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>聽勒</rb><rt>Tʽing-leh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>扭帶轉</rb><rt>nyin-tæn-cün</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩位</rb><rt>liang-we</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>廿五</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>事幹過</rb><rt>z-ken-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>Zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>錯</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶花個</rb><rt>na-hwun-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。 〈<ruby><rb>馬太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby>〉21: 16. 

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>八歲</rb><rt>pah-shü</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>抱其個</rb><rt>bao-gyi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>前</rb><rt>Zin</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一落過了</rb><rt>ih-lôh-ko-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>企慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>早了</rb><rt>tsao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>一起</rb><rt>ih-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>學生子</rb><rt>ʽoh-sang-ts</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>歸去個</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>意</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>思</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得個</rb><rt>s-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>走出去了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>書房</rb><rt>shü-vông</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前頭個</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>Kao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>姑娘</rb><rt>kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>半年</rb><rt>pun-nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>菩薩個</rb><rt>bu-sah-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>棄邪歸正</rb><rt>kyʽi-zia-kwe-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>難得個</rb><rt>næn-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>錯</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>假話個</rb><rt>kô-wô-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾句</rb><rt>kyi-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講起來</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>縛住了</rb><rt>bo-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>輕</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>忖起來</rb><rt>tsʽeng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>心心</rb><rt>sing-sing</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讀點</rb><rt>doh-tin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽聽</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>法</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>兩章</rb><rt>liang-tsông</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>創世記</rb><rt>Tsʽông-shü-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>兩章</rb>
