<rt>liang-tsông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>Tʽin-di</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百樣個</rb><rt>pah-yiang-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>亞當</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt><rb>夏娃</rb><rt>Yüô-w̆ô</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>救世主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Shü-Cü</rt><rb>降落來個</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兩章</rb><rt>liang-tsông</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>Foh-ing</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>翻來</rb><rt>fæn-læ</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>學會了</rb><rt>ʽôh-we-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>肯個</rb><rt>Kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>書房裏</rb><rt>shü-vông-li</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt><rb>國裏</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>波斯個</rb><rt>Po-s-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇頭個</rb><rt>Keh-deo-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>究竟</rb><rt>kyiu-kying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>時</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>蒲齊個</rb><rt>Bu-zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。《<ruby><rb>聖書》個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>憂悶了</rb><rt>iu-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>開導</rb><rt>kʽæ-dao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總是</rb><rt>tsong-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滋潤</rb><rt>ts-jing</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>抽芽出來</rb><rt>tsʽiu-ngô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>扎秧子</rb><rt>tsah-iang-ts</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>正是介</rb><rt>tsing-z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>走出來個</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>走轉來</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>辦個</rb><rt>bæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>成功個</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt></ruby>。（〈<ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby>〉55: 10–13.）<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>–</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>蒲</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>齊講</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>個說</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>話，好</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>甮等</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>辯</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>駁；因</rb><rt>bin-poh</rt><rb>爲爾</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>紀輕</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt><rb>未</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>點了</rb><rt>mi-tin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書》撥</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>求神</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>明，就</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>住勒</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>加勒葛搭</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-keh-tah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>年多星</rb><rt>nyin-to-sing</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>認</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>翻譯</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>訂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好仔</rb><rt>hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>裝潢</rb><rt>tsông-wông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>紅簿面</rb><rt>ʽong-bu-min</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>一時裏</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>抄</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>字頭</rb><rt>z-den</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>和攏</rb><rt>ʽo-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書》個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>心裏頭</rb><rt>sing-li-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>比如</rb><rt>pi-jü</rt><rb>種田個</rb><rt>cong-din-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>慢慢個</rb><rt>mæn-mæn-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>抽出來</rb><rt>tsʽiu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>過仔</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>號個</rb><rt>ʽao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸趣向</rb><rt>m-cʽü-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>並弗是</rb><rt>Bing-feh-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生個</rb><rt>sin-sang-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>加勒葛搭</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-keh-tah</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>遊戲個</rb><rt>yiu-hyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>mæ</rt><rb>時道個</rb><rt>z-dao-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>便當</rb><rt>bin-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>彎</rb><rt>w̆æn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>書個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原文印爲 Kyüô-leh-keh-teh，前文則爲 Kyüô-leh-keh-tah</sup>

<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>Zo</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>一邊</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>撐</rb><rt>tsʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一邊</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>用心</rb><rt>yüong-sing</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書》裏個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>熱</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>肝氣痛個</rb><rt>ken-kyʽi-tʽong-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>發了</rb><rt>fah-de</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>粥</rb><rt>coh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>瘦</rb><rt>seo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>撐</rb><rt>tsʽang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>行醫</rb><rt>ʽang-i</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>風涼個</rb><rt>fong-liang-go</rt><rb>屋裏頭</rb><rt>oh-li-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>船裏頭</rb><rt>jün-li-deo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>熱</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>撐勒</rb><rt>tsʽang-leh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>撐勒</rb><rt>tsʽang-leh</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>厲害了</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原文印爲 Bô-ken-pô，前後文均爲 Bô-hen-pô</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>Keh-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>河邊沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>Jün</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一看見</rb><rt>Ih-kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>仔細</rb><rt>Ts-si</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>𢞕</rb><rt>hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>面色</rb><rt>Min-seh</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>請請</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>哦</rb><rt>ô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥幹</rb><rt>soh-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>體面個</rb><rt>tʽi-min-go</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>阿姆</rb><rt>A-m</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>亂出來</rb><rt>lön-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>Kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稍爲</rb><rt>sao-we</rt><rb>清爽點</rb><rt>tsʽing-sông-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>Zo</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>場</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>軟弱個</rb><rt>nyün-ziah-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爽快</rb><rt>shông-kwʽa</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>魂靈</rb><rt>weng-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母個</rb><rt>s-meo-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母個</rb><rt>s-meo-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt><rb>春凳</rb><rt>cʽing-teng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>夜裏</rb><rt>yia-li</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>隔壁</rb><rt>kah-pih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>師母</rb><rt>S-meo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>病裏</rb><rt>bing-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>辦到</rb><rt>bæn-tao</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>住勒</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>由</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>心心</rb><rt>sing-sing</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗在乎</rb><rt>feh-dzæ-vu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>門份個</rb><rt>meng-veng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>用心</rb><rt>yüong-sing</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>個個</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>藥</rb><rt>yiah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>點心</rb><rt>tin-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>輕</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>暴時</rb><rt>bao-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>風涼</rb><rt>fong-liang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偶湊</rb><rt>ngeo-tsʽeo</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>房子裏</rb><rt>vông-ts-li</rt><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>兩篇</rb><rt>liang-pʽin</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>軟弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>小官人</rb><rt>siao-kwun-nying</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走開</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>去看</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>永生</rb><rt>üong-sang</rt><rb>永世</rb><rt>üong-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忘記</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>結煞</rb><rt>Kyih-sah</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>轎子</rb><rt>gyiao-ts</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>墳田</rb><rt>veng-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>睏來</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>墳裏頭</rb><rt>veng-li-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>魂靈</rb><rt>weng-ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>愛敬個</rb><rt>æ-kying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>心滿</rb><rt>sing-mun</rt><rb>意足了</rb><rt>i-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。」（〈<ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby>〉17: 16.）<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>Se-tsih</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>皮肉</rb><rt>bi-nyüoh</rt><rb>爛糊個</rb><rt>læn-wu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><sup> 2 </sup><ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>囡囡</rb><rt>Nön-nön</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>候氣力</rb><rt>ʽeo-kyʽi-lih</rt><rb>應</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>撐</rb><rt>tsʽang</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>合帶攏</rb><rt>ʽeh-tæn-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心裏頭個</rb><rt>sing-li-deo-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡個</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>Tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>寶貝個</rb><rt>pao-pe-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>罪過</rb><rt>ze-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>換</rb><rt>wun</rt><rb>一副</rb><rt>ih-fu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>免過</rb><rt>min-ko</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>感謝</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>盡個</rb><rt>zing-go</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>睏弗熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-feh-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>一長日</rb><rt>ih-dziang-nyih</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>犯罪個</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小來</rb><rt>siao-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>方纔</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恆沙河</rb><rt>ʽEng-sô-ʽo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>滰弗</rb><rt>gyiang-feh</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>軟弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撐</rb><rt>tsʽang</rt><rb>弗起來</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也< rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>捨得</rb><rt>sô-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地獄裏</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>求求個</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能</rb><rt>neng</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>照應</rb><rt>tsiao-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>聽見</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惶恐勒緊</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-leh-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>捫住</rb><rt>meng-djü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>歇了</rb><rt>Hyih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歇了</rb><rt>hyih-de</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>講出來</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>省掉</rb><rt>sang-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>聽見</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講完</rb><rt>kông-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>剪刀</rb><rt>tsin-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帽子</rb><rt>mao-ts</rt><rb>一摘</rb><rt>ih-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>軟軟</rb><rt>nyün-nyün</rt><rb>光光個</rb><rt>kwông-kwông-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>披落來</rb><rt>pʽi-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>弗住</rb><rt>feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>剪刀</rb><rt>tsin-tao</rt><rb>放落</rb><rt>fông-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嗐</rb><rt>ʽÆ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>寶貝個</rb><rt>pao-pe-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>弗住</rb><rt>feh-djü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>去弗得個</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>後頭個</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>值鈿</rb><rt>dzih-din</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>總會</rb><rt>tsong-we</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>Tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>講完</rb><rt>kông-wun</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>着實</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>對</rb><rt>te</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>藥</rb><rt>yiah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-nyiang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>半夜過</rb><rt>Pun-yia-ko</rt><rb>有點</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>醒轉來</rb><rt>sing-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>問起</rb><rt>meng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>閒常</rb><rt>ʽÆn-djông</rt><rb>別日</rb><rt>bih-nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>問起個</rb><rt>meng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇時</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>病勢</rb><rt>bing-shü</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聽見</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>㪗起來</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>要緊</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>半日</rb><rt>pun-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖來</rb><rt>tsʽeng-læ</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>Shih-wô</rt><rb>講完</rb><rt>kông-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>眼泡皮</rb><rt>ngæn-pʽao-bi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>腫腫個</rb><rt>cong-cong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>房裏</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>一點</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>讀起</rb><rt>doh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>禮拜日</rb><rt>Li-pa-nyih</rt><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>當值</rb><rt>tông-dzih</rt><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出材</rb><rt>cʽih-zæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>送送</rb><rt>song-song</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石碑牌</rb><rt>zah-pe-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>碑牌上</rb><rt>Pe-ba-zông</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>姓數</rb><rt>sing-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt></ruby><sup> 1 </sup><ruby><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書》裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>Sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」（〈<ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby>〉5: 24.）

<sup>\[1\] 此處原文錯印爲 dah</sup>

<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石碑上</rb><rt>zah-pe-zông</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt><rb>一節</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>》，「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>若有</rb><rt>ziah-yiu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>真道理</rb><rt>tsing-dao-li</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>岔路個</rb><rt>tsʽô-lu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>岔路</rb><rt>tsʽô-lu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>死路</rb><rt>si-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby>〉v: 8. 

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>一回</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>琶漢巴</rb><rt>Bô-hen-pô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>Kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>石碑牌</rb><rt>zah-pe-ba</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雪刮</rb><rt>shih-kwah</rt><rb>亮個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>仔</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>風雨</rb><rt>fong-yü</rt><rb>弄掉頭</rb><rt>long-diao-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>筆劃</rb><rt>pih-wah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清楚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天堂</rb><rt>Tʽin-dông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>隊</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>庉間</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>。《<ruby><rb>聖書》裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>現在個</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>世界</rb><rt>shü-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>私慾</rb><rt>s-yüoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」 〈<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby>〉2: 17. 

<ruby><rb>顯理</rb><rt>Hyin-li</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裴</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>師母</rb><rt>s-meo</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顯理個</rb><rt>Hyin-li-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>封來</rb><rt>fong-læ</rt><rb>信裏頭</rb><rt>sing-li-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>史</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt><rb>佛教</rb><rt>Vah-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮個</rb><rt>si-li-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>改</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>嘸幾</rb><rt>m-kyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>蒲齊</rb><rt>Bu-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>智慧個</rb><rt>Cü-we-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>顯</rb><rt>Hyin</rt><rb>理</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>學</rb><rt>ʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。
